## 20. THE CENTRAL MYSTERY — "THE POINT" 🔒

The player chose "surprise me completely." This is the spine that will entangle the ensemble from the pilot through at
least S5, with consequences through the full run.

### 20.1 The surface (what Marisol believes)
At about 6:10 a.m. on **Sunday, April 26, 2009** (the Third Sunday of Easter), two surfers paddling out at the Point break
found the body of **Nora Grace Calloway**, 19, a sophomore, on the rocks below the old Point Marisol Light. The coroner
found a BAC of 0.09 and a therapeutic level of alprazolam. Her phone was in her jeans, destroyed. Marisol PD (Det. Sgt.
Ray Ochoa) ruled it an accidental fall: she was known to sit on the seaward side of the low wall at night. The campus held
a vigil. St. Brendan's held a memorial Mass. A white wooden cross stands at the wall, and every Sunday before dawn
someone leaves fresh flowers there; nobody knows who. Privately, many — including her parents' parish in Selma, the
detective, Julian, and later Margot — believe it was suicide.

### 20.2 Who she was
Nora Grace Calloway, born **September 8, 1989** (the Nativity of Mary) in Selma, California. Father Tom, a raisin farmer;
mother Rosa (Gutiérrez), a school-district bookkeeper; two younger brothers (Mateo, 15; Sam, 12). Philosophy major, Music
minor, Newman choir soprano. Funny, generous, intense, devout, doubting, brave. Freshman year in Delgado 222 with Priya;
sophomore year in Serra 3B with Sloane. She wore a Levi's jean jacket with a **Fresno Fair pin** on the collar and an
embroidered **Our Lady of Guadalupe patch** sewn on the back by her grandmother. She sang with Julian at the Point wall.
She sent Hale a rebuttal in high school and got his postcard. She took his seminar. She fell in love with his TA.

### 20.3 The truth of the night (timeline)
- **Oct 2008–Apr 2009:** Nora and Evan Marsh, secretly together.
- **Sat Apr 25, 2:14 p.m.:** Walgreens on Mission Street: pregnancy test. Positive (about 8 weeks).
- **6:00–8:00 p.m.:** Newman admit dinner (Nora hosts Lena).
- **~8:30 p.m.:** Nora texts Evan the news. He calls; they fight — he panics, says "we can't," says "there are options."
- **10:30 p.m.:** Nora goes to the Lighthouse party (the last house on Seacliff Road, rented by senior surfers). Julian is
  there with his guitar; they sing one song on the stairs ("Skinny Love"). Rafi Ochoa, a high-school senior then, is
  selling. Nora buys two Xanax bars from him: she hasn't slept in a week. She takes one with a beer. Sloane sees her leave
  at ~10:50, crying.
- **Also at the Lighthouse:** Theo Vance, 18, an admit, crashing with an LA friend's older brother who lives there. Around
  10:45 he goes down the bluff stairs to the beach to smoke and meets **Kevin Tran**, 18, an admit, who wandered out of
  the party because he couldn't breathe. They talk for an hour. They kiss. Kevin leaves ~11:50 for his host's room in
  Serra Hall, shaking with joy and terror. Theo stays, does two lines of cocaine alone on the stairs, and walks out along
  the bluff toward the light.
- **11:15 p.m.:** Nora back at Serra; the bathroom; the Memorare. **11:40:** "Cover for me, Magdalena." **11:45:** Lena sees
  the test.
- **11:52 p.m.:** On the Point path, Nora calls Hale. Forty-one seconds. *"Go home, Nora. You're drunk and you're boring
  me."*
- **12:05 a.m.:** Nora texts Evan: *"I'm at the Point. Please come. I'm keeping it. I want to keep it. I want you to want
  it."* She sits on the seaward side of the wall, legs over the drop, as she has done a hundred times, and prays aloud.
- **~12:25 a.m.:** Theo, high, comes up the path in the dark and sees a girl on the outside of the wall, talking to
  someone who isn't there. He thinks she's about to jump. He runs at her and grabs her from behind by the jacket. She
  startles, twists, her hand slips off the wall; the jacket pulls off her shoulders as she goes; he's left holding it. She
  doesn't scream. He hears her hit. The last words he heard her say were *"…never was it known that anyone who fled to thy
  protection…"*
- **~12:27–12:31:** Theo, in shock, runs. Down the Point path toward Seacliff Road, the jacket clutched to his chest.
- **12:20:** Evan reads the text and drives. **12:31:** at the Point lot his headlights catch a tall blond boy running
  toward Seacliff Road holding something dark. Evan goes to the wall; it's black; he calls her phone (it's under her, in
  the surf, already dead); he waits ten minutes, decides she's left to punish him, drives home. He deletes the text the
  next morning. He keeps a screenshot on his laptop he took at 12:21 to "show her later how scared he'd been." It's still
  there.
- **~12:45 a.m.:** Theo walks to Serra Hall — the only place on campus he knows anyone — and knocks on Kevin's host's door
  (Serra 3F, two doors from Nora's room), soaked to the knees from crossing the creek mouth, palms scraped raw from the
  path, holding the jacket. He's crying. *"You never saw me."* Kevin lets him in; he sleeps on the floor; he's gone at
  dawn. **This is the crying boy Lena heard.**
- **6:10 a.m.:** surfers find her.

### 20.4 The layers of guilt (no villains)
Every person who touched that night believes, privately, that they killed Nora Calloway:
- **Theo** — he did, by trying to save her. The truest guilt and the least culpable intent.
- **Evan** — he said "there are options," didn't answer at 12:05, saw the boy, drove away, lied.
- **Hale** — forty-one seconds.
- **Lena** — she covered for her; she didn't stop her; she hid the test; she didn't look at the crying boy.
- **Rafi** — the Xanax.
- **Kevin** — he let Theo in and never asked.
- **Julian** — he didn't see she was drowning.
- **Sloane** — she let her walk out of the party crying.
- **Priya** — they drifted.
- **Ray Ochoa** — he closed it as an accident while believing it was suicide, out of mercy, and that mercy kept the truth
  buried.

The show's answer, delivered over seasons and never as a speech: everyone did a small wrong thing, the fall was an
accident, and Nora **was not trying to die** — she had chosen to keep her child and was praying the Memorare on the wall
where she always prayed. The proof is two things only a guilty man and a coward kept: Evan's screenshot of the text, and
Theo's memory of the prayer. The truth heals one person completely — Rosa Calloway, her mother, who has believed for two
years that her daughter killed herself and her grandchild. In S5, Rosa receives Communion for the first time since April
2009. That's the payoff the whole mystery is built for.

### 20.5 Physical evidence and clues
- **The jean jacket** (Theo's closet, in a black Prada duffel on the top shelf). Levi's, women's small, Fresno Fair pin
  on the collar, Guadalupe patch on the back. Inside pocket: **Hale's 2007 postcard to Nora** (Radcliffe Camera; "Come and
  say it to my face. —A.H."), folded soft; a Walgreens receipt (Apr 25, 2:14 p.m.: First Response, $16.99); a St. Brendan's
  choir schedule. Police report notes "victim reportedly wearing a denim jacket; not recovered; presumed lost to the sea."
- **Phone records:** 11:52 p.m. call to Hale (41 sec); 12:05 a.m. text to Evan Marsh. Ochoa interviewed both. Hale said she
  was upset about a paper. Evan said he was asleep and saw the text in the morning. Content of the text never retrieved.
- **Evan's laptop:** the 12:21 screenshot.
- **The cross and the Sunday flowers:** Theo. Every Sunday before dawn. Supermarket roses in winter; jasmine from Las Olas
  fences in summer.
- **Nora's journal** (Priya's box): last entry April 23: *"If I tell him and he says no, I'll still say yes. Lord, to whom
  shall we go."* (John 6:68.) Priya opens it in S2.
- **Julian's song "Fresno":** a line, *"you said the wall was where you yelled at God / I never heard him yell back"* —
  and a verse about her jacket.
- **The postcards:** Jack's, Lena's, Nora's. Same line, same handwriting, same Radcliffe Camera image. Hale's habit.
- **Lena's memory:** the boy crying in the corridor at Serra Hall; a door two down the hall.
- **Kevin's Preview Weekend host:** Serra 3F, two doors from 3B. In the housing records Margot pulls (S3).
- **Evan's sighting:** tall, blond, running, carrying something dark.
- **The Lighthouse:** the senior surfers rotate out; in Fall 2009 it's rented by a new crew who throw the season-opening
  party (the pilot). A photo from April 25 on a Facebook album ("LIGHTHOUSE LAST HURRAH 4/25") shows Theo in the
  background, by the stairs, at 10:41 p.m. Margot finds it in S3.

### 20.6 Red herrings (fair, each true about something)
1. **Hale did it** (S2): the postcard in the jacket points at him; his drinking, his possessiveness, his lie about the
   call. He didn't push her; he did fail her.
2. **Suicide** (S1–S2): the pregnancy, the Xanax, the crying, "cover for me," the town's whisper. Wrong — and the most
   cruel wrong.
3. **Evan pushed her** (S2–S3): he was there; he lied; he's unstable. He didn't.
4. **Julian** (S2, briefly; Jack's jealousy makes him want it): he loved her, he wrote the song, he knows the wall. No.
5. **Sloane / Pi Lam hazing** (S1E12, Laney's arc): a rumor the sorority made pledges walk the wall; false, though the
   rumor is real and leads to the Halloween scare.
6. **Rafi's pills killed her** (S2): the Xanax made her unsteady. Partly true; not the cause.

### 20.7 Seed plan — what lands when
**Pilot (S1E01) — invisible seeds only:**
- Priya's floor-meeting rule: nobody goes to the Point after dark.
- At the end of the Lighthouse driveway, a man older than everyone, smoking, staring at the house, not coming in (Evan).
  Gone later.
- Theo and Kevin's "first" handshake held a second too long; Theo quiet for one line; Kevin's smile.
- Rafi: "Not bars, though. I don't do bars anymore."
- Julian on the stairs with a guitar; Lena stopping to listen with an expression Jack can't read.
- On the back porch Lena knows the gap in the fence and the path to the Point and warns Jack off it; when he asks how she
  knows, she says Laney's sorority friend told her — and touches her medal.
- Jack's paperback drops Hale's postcard; Lena reads it; something happens in her face; she asks "Who's A.H.?"; Jack
  deflects. (She has the same card.)
- If Jack goes to the cove with Theo: Theo, looking north at the dark cliff, asks about hell and the exchange rate on
  forgiveness. Theo leaves with Sloane.
- Mrs. Fitch: "We lost our best soprano last spring."
- After Mass, Lena lights a candle at the Marian shrine beside a small brass plate: NORA GRACE CALLOWAY · 1989–2009 · "LORD,
  TO WHOM SHALL WE GO?"
- Osei to Lena: "Magdalena. You came back."
- Tag: Theo comes in before dawn with sand on his feet and wet hems. "Couldn't sleep. Went to look at the ocean."
- 🔒 It is Sunday morning. He was leaving the flowers.

**S1 seeds and turns:**
- E02 (re-planned after S1E01): Hale's first seminar — Phineas Gage ("Gage was no longer Gage"; Jack's hand goes to the
  eyebrow; 🔒 Mikey); if Jack's heart answer, John 6:68 aloud (the plaque's words; Hale: "I was made to learn it"). Caleb:
  "He wrote you *back*? I wrote him four letters" — Lena silent beside them. Hale recognizes the girl who fled the Anchor
  (Lena's medal lie: "something on the stove"). Evan is the driveway man and the Thursday TA; if Jack mentions the
  Lighthouse, Evan says he doesn't go to parties and asks only who Jack came with. Facebook "People You May Know": a candle,
  "Nora Calloway · 1 mutual friend (Priya Raman)" (no Lena, Julian or Sloane anywhere on it). Wes: a status about the Army
  Corps of Engineers; a "like" at 3:58 a.m. Central; the first missed 8:04 call. Kevin's smooth lie ("Met him at the floor
  meeting"). Rudy's mother, three buses every other Sunday (🔒 rhymes with Maria and Danny). Doug last out at the alarm.
- E03 (re-planned after S1E02; Labor Day weekend in Malibu): **Theo keeps the Sunday flowers even from Malibu** — the SL
  is gone from the drive before dawn Sunday (Margot, who doesn't sleep, saw it leave at 2:40) and back by the end of the
  8 a.m. Mass, dew on the paint, Theo outside the church with coffees: "I'm a supporter, not a member" (🔒 three hours up,
  the flowers, three hours back; and he has been inside St. Brendan's exactly once, in July). The pilot's unasked cove
  question is re-seeded in Theo's idiom when Jack pays him the pie: "Does He take IOUs? Your God. Or is it cash on
  delivery." Theo hears Danny's call and keeps it — the first secret Theo keeps *for* Jack (🔒 the mirror: the keeper of the
  worst secret on campus is good at it). Kevin and Theo at opposite ends of the pool at 1 a.m., feet in the water, not
  talking (one image; no comment). Jack's paperback is *Brideshead Revisited*; Margot: "You know what that book is about."
  "Grace." "Men in love with each other in a very large house." (Both right; 🔒 Theo.)
- E04 (re-planned after S1E03; full plan in `outlines/s1e04.md`) (Tue Sept 8, Nativity of Mary — Nora's 20th birthday):
  Lena absent from **PHIL 1, Row M** (🔒 she walked to the Point at sunrise and sat at the wall with the Memorare until the
  fog lifted; nobody saw her); at the 12:10 she lights a candle at the Marian shrine above the plate — **Jack: "Happy
  birthday to her." (Our Lady.) Lena: "To who?"** (medal) — and **Mrs. Fitch sets a white rose beside the plate**. **Hale
  drinks alone at the Anchor that night (12:38 a.m.), recites Housman (ASL LIV), and dismisses Jack with "Go home, Mr.
  Costigan. You're sober and you're boring me"** — 🔒 nearly the forty-one seconds verbatim; never juxtapose. Wednesday
  **Hale wears a clean jacket**. **Priya** at 1:46 a.m. with a cupcake and a two-for-one balloon (its twin at the cross)
  tells Jack: her freshman roommate, Delgado 222, Selma, soprano, fell "end of April," Tuesday would have been twenty;
  **"She prayed the Memorare in the shower"** (🔒 the last words Theo heard); "People say other things" (red herring #2).
  **Journal: Nora becomes a person card.** At the cross (Point fork): **browned jasmine tied with kitchen string** (🔒 Theo's
  Sunday flowers from the 2:40 Malibu drive) and the balloon's twin; Julian at the wall. Thursday: **Evan cancels section —
  "PERSONAL"**; **Julian sings "Fresno"** at Café Bastille ("you said the wall was where you yelled at God / I never heard
  Him yell back"); his voice goes before the last verse (🔒 the jacket verse — never sung on the page in S1); **Lena leaves in
  the second chorus** and lies about the air (medal). The Odyssey's Saints countdown sits at 6 DAYS Tue–Wed (she didn't
  update it) and jumps to 2 DAYS Friday.
- E04 **played as planned** on this path except: the Point fork was not taken (iftar), so the jasmine and Julian at the
  wall were **not seen**; Priya's candle branch (no "seaward side"); "Fresno" heard to the second chorus only (Jack left
  with Lena; the third-verse break was not witnessed).
- E05 (re-planned after S1E04; full plan in `outlines/s1e05.md`): **Hale's two drivers** — *"Two men drive home from the
  same party. Only one of them hits anybody."* (Nagel's case; moral luck is Hale's own D.Phil. subject — 🔒 the forty-one
  seconds). Jack argues the unlucky driver must answer, and "nobody gets to take it for him" (🔒 Danny); Lena argues the lucky
  one is just as guilty (🔒 she holds herself guilty for a night in which she "did nothing"). Each argues against themselves;
  neither knows the other is. Hale to Lena: *"Forgiven by whom? The only one with standing to forgive is the one who can't."*
  (🔒 Nora.) Hale to Jack, after: *"Do you think the lucky one sleeps, Mr. Costigan?"* Monday 3 a.m., for Swayze: Theo on
  *Point Break* — "Utah lets him go. Nicest thing anybody does in the whole movie." (🔒 Theo grabbed.) **The jacket: Thursday
  ~1:10 a.m. (Wed night), 412** — Jack's RAZR is dying, the charger is behind the Anchor's Hobart; Theo, back from the shower,
  says "Top shelf. The gray bag"; Jack pulls the black one. A women's Levi's jacket, small; when it unfolds, an embroidered
  Our Lady of Guadalupe on the back, roses at her feet. **The narration notes the patch and nothing else — not the pin.** Theo
  takes it out of his hands (the fastest thing he does all week): *"A girl left it."* — then too much detail (Brentwood,
  Marymount, Prague for the semester, nobody has an address in Prague): the bible's tell, unflagged. No Nora beat that day
  or that night. Thursday: **Evan's "third driver"** in section — "the one who stops, looks, sees nothing, and drives home"
  (🔒 12:31 a.m.); no gloss. Kevin (fork B only): "The one who hit somebody always walks out without a scratch" (about drunk
  drivers in the ER; Jack's secret, not the Point).
- E05 **played as planned** on this path: pref fork (no Kevin ER line; no telling), RIGOR win with ferocity ("nobody takes
  it for him"), "It's about my brother," the jacket seen (patch only; "Hope she's warm in Prague"), Danny met Lena on the
  bench. Evan's third driver heard (Jack asked "What does that one owe?" — "Purity of heart… I've never managed two").
- E06 (re-planned after S1E05; full plan in `outlines/s1e06.md`): **Rafi's father is a cop** — Det. Sgt. Ray Ochoa's
  "License and registration" bit on Ocean Road; **he does not open his son's bag** (🔒 mercy, the case). Rafi re-plants **"Not
  bars, though. I don't do bars anymore."** **Lena hears "Ochoa"** in the two-hander and touches the medal (🔒 her statement).
  **The creek mouth** (the Lagoon's outlet across the beach below the Grove): Jack and Lena wade it at ~2:15 a.m. on the long
  way home (🔒 Theo crossed it, soaked to the knees, at ~12:40 a.m. April 26) — no gloss. **Theo at the fire alarm carries
  both bags, the black one too, and leaves the espresso machine** (🔒). **Sunday dawn: "I run now"** (🔒 the flowers; over-
  specific). **Lena turns east** from the Pi Lam steps without discussing it (🔒 away from the Point; never remarked). **"Why
  Marisol?" "Money."** (medal; 🔒 Nora, "I owe her four years"); Jack: "You know why I came" (the postcard; 🔒 she has the
  same card and says nothing — the E14 discovery stays intact). Hale: **"Miss Broussard was here at two"** — Lena tells Jack
  she went "to tell him he was wrong about who gets to forgive" (🔒 Nora) and no more. Peter: "we haven't had to find out"
  (🔒 Peter & Kenzie). Margot watching Kevin at "your future wife" (🔒 E07). Nora's name is not spoken in E06; Priya appears
  only as a laminate.
- E06 **played as planned** on this path except: the Pallbearer fork was not taken (Margot's Friday and Gina's text read
  upside down unseen; Jack heard no wake invitation — E07's funeral card is his first); no "What do you do when you fall?"
  (Peter's lie unheard; Kenzie's "Good question" unseen); Wes's three tells not named (`e6_wes=="fine"`). Landed: Theo's two
  bags; Ray and the unopened bag; "Not bars"; Lena's medal at "Ochoa"; the creek mouth; "Money"; "Miss Broussard was here at
  two" / "who gets to forgive"; "I run now."
- E07 (re-planned after S1E06; full plan in `outlines/s1e07.md`): **Theo keeps away from the parapet** all night on the roof
  (films from the elevator housing; never within a body's length of the edge — 🔒 the wall; no gloss). **Theo hands Kevin the
  5D** to hold while he lifts Laney; Kevin holds it as if it were hot (🔒 April; one image, no comment). **Kevin in a plain gray
  T-shirt** — no quarter-zip (the motif: something is very wrong; 🔒 he is about to tell Margot). **Lena counts the pills in
  Laney's DRP pillbox** while Laney sleeps and puts it back (🔒 S3; Jack sees; no gloss). **Margot's committal "to the deep"**
  (she reads the Prayer Book's burial at sea over a Mason jar "for the prose") — Theo lowers the camera; no gloss (🔒). **Lena's
  too-specific line** in the argument — when a girl decides to keep it, "everybody has an opinion and nobody has a crib" —
  medal (🔒 Nora: "I'm keeping it"). At dawn **"You argued that like it was yours"** (Jack turns her own E05 line) → medal;
  "It's everybody's" (🔒 a lie-shaped truth). **Evan on Kierkegaard's preferential love** ("the self loved twice") in section
  (🔒 Nora; no gloss). **Mark 9:43, Sunday** — Jack's right hand (🔒 the punch; elision). **Nora's name is not spoken in E07**;
  no plaque business beyond Lena lighting a candle for Nonna at the shrine.
- E07 **played as planned** on this path (who · coming · pecan · smoke · press · lena · flannel · yours · noticed · okay ·
  RIGOR/Lewis · sleep · margot · cite · go · twoam · kiss · stairs · becca · boyfriend · sullys). Landed: Theo and the parapet;
  Kevin and the camera; Kevin's plain T-shirt; Lena counting the pills; the committal "to the deep"; "nobody has a crib" (medal);
  "It's everybody's" (medal); Evan on preferential love and Regine Olsen; Mark 9:43 and the right hand on his knee (Sully's path,
  9 p.m. Mass). Not landed: Wes and Luc (HEART verdict not reached).
- E08 (re-planned after S1E07 for the player's two-hander design; full plan in `outlines/s1e08.md`) — light, all 🔒, no gloss:
  **Theo on St. Michael** (Tue, 412): Jack: he throws the devil out of heaven. Theo: **"Does he catch anybody? Or just throw?"**
  (🔒 the grab at the wall.) **Hale's lying seminar**: "The commonest lie in the world… is told to policemen, about the dead, by
  people who loved them" — **Lena's medal** (🔒 her statement to Ochoa); Hale looks at the window (🔒 his own). **Danny's "Happy St.
  Mike's"** (🔒 sponsor). **The Thérèse duel's Personal option** (path only): "You picked a saint who never had to lie to anybody"
  — Lena goes formal for a beat (🔒; she makes him apologize, it's her feast). **Hale's "And with thy spirit"** at the Blessing (🔒
  Compline; Jack notices the words are wrong, not why). **Theo under Osei's aspergillum** at the Blessing lets the water hit him
  and shuts his eyes (🔒 July's confession; one clause). At Shipwreck Cove, if Jack suggests walking on toward the headland for
  the view, Lena says no without a reason and starts back the other way (🔒 the Point; no remark; one line). Nora's name is **not** spoken; no jacket; no Evan beat (section off-page); Rafi/Ray absent.
- E09: Wes and Jack on the beach. Not the mystery — but the theme (the knocking no one answered).
- E11: Priya shows Jack the box; he sees the journal; she won't open it.
- E12 (Halloween): Evan sees Theo for the first time at the Las Olas chaos; goes white. Laney's pledge "Point walk" rumor;
  Jack finds Theo at the Point wall at 2 a.m., drunk, looking down. "Don't. Okay? Just — not there."
- E13 (All Souls): the Book of the Dead: Lena writes *Lucille Guidry* and *Nora Calloway*. Jack sees. "She was my host at
  Preview Weekend." True; partial.
- E14: the postcards: Jack and Lena discover they both have Hale's card.
- E16: the Kevin/Theo stairwell fight Jack half-hears: "You said you'd never—" "I haven't."
- E19: Evan asks Jack, "Your roommate — was he at Preview Weekend last April?"
- E20: the jacket again — Jack notices the Fresno Fair pin.
- **E22 (season cliffhanger):** On the last night before break, Lena shows Jack a photo on her laptop: Nora at the Point
  wall, laughing, in her jean jacket — the Guadalupe patch, the Fresno Fair pin. "They never found it. Her mom asked me in
  a letter if I remembered what she was wearing." Jack has seen that jacket. Smash to black.

**S2 (Spring 2010):** Jack checks the closet — the duffel is gone. Theo has moved it (to his mother's pool house in La
Cañada; later back). Jack investigates in silence with Margot (who is starting the anniversary feature) and alone; he
finds the postcard in the jacket (E10) → Hale suspicion; the anniversary at the Point (E18, Apr 25–26, 2010): Evan
confronts Theo at the cross before dawn with the flowers in his hand; Jack intervenes; Evan knows now. **S2E22:** Theo
tells Jack the truth on the wall. Jack, in exchange — because it's the only thing he can offer — tells Theo about Danny.
(The player learns both truths in the same scene.)

**S3 (Fall 2010):** Jack holds Theo's secret while Theo tries to get clean; Margot dates Evan and finds the screenshot;
Lena confides (E18) about the test — to Jack, who can't tell her what he knows. Laney's overdose (Nov 2010) on Theo's
cocaine. Kevin comes out to Jack and Lena.

**S4 (Spring 2011):** Jack and Lena together, with the secret between them. Margot's reporting closes in. Evan goes to the
Calloways. Kevin decides to testify. **April 24, 2011, Easter Sunday:** Theo goes to Mass with Jack. **April 26, 2011:**
Theo walks into Marisol PD with the jacket and Ray Ochoa takes his statement. Lena learns Jack knew for a year. The
breakup.

**S5 (Fall 2011):** consequences. The DA's decision (the grab was a rescue attempt; the flight is the problem; drug
possession; a misdemeanor plea and community service — the law shrugs, and the shrug is its own horror for Theo). The
press realizes Fr. Osei may have known; the seal on trial; Osei jailed for contempt. Rosa Calloway receives Communion.

**Full run:** the case's after-life — Theo's film, Evan's prison teaching, Ray Ochoa's retirement, the cross at the
Point removed by the county in 2014 and replaced by a bench with a plaque that Theo pays for anonymously: *"Never was it
known."*

gave her the last word; he let her know he was on the stairs. #2 seeded (all pilot seeds landed; cove seed not seen — Theo's
"exchange rate" question unasked on this path, re-seed it in E03 or E12). #3 seeded — Danny **not** named at Marisol.

not met Jack (he followed Lena out); first meeting E02. #13/#14 Margot: "And every father" — she's opened a file; she coins
"useful idiots" aloud in E02 (whiteboard still E04). #16 seeded (MEMORIAL; "Mama said don't"). #28 starts E02. #29 one
cigarette in her pocket. #30 tellings: Theo swan; Lena pierogi + Archbishop; Osei "a fight" (honest, partial). #32 Anchor

**Status after S1E02 (played path):** #1 open — the endgame groove is forming early and warm: trust 7, warmth 11, heat 4,
friction 6. Jack sided with her against Margot, formed a "union" with her against Hale, told her "Bleed on mine," said
she'd had him worried about her taste (Wes), offered her the whole pack (she refused: one at a time, "off your face"),
handed her the flask without drinking. Season duel record **2–0 Jack** (E02 RIGOR); her line "I owe you a Sunday, Costigan.
